# All 288 direct binomial-residue formulas
Author: Komorebi P.

Record identifiers agree with the rational formula catalogue. Each entry gives the complete finite polynomial part $S(x)$ and each lane's period $d$, multiplicity $e$, positive scale $h$ and integer coefficient list $c_i$ (ascending powers of $x$).

For $n\ge0$ set $q=\lfloor n/d\rfloor$, $r=n\bmod d$. For each lane add

$$\frac1h\sum_{j=0}^{\min(q,e-1)}c_{r+dj}\binom{q-j+e-1}{e-1}.$$

Then add the coefficient $S_n$, zero if outside the finite part. The resulting integer is the exact count at vertex index $n$. In each lane's coefficient blocks, `i-j` means the listed integers are $c_i,\ldots,c_j$ in order; omitted higher coefficients are zero. The 196 v32 and 47 v31 rooted/unrooted lane records are copied from their archives. The 45 v31 branching-profile lane records were compiled for this edition using the verified v32 exact polynomial-CRT routine and checked against their stored terms.
